ama_initialize_effect = {
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = ama_rt_ticket } } set_variable = { name = ama_rt_ticket value = 0 } }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = ama_rt_phase } } set_variable = { name = ama_rt_phase value = 0 } }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = ama_fee_percent } } set_variable = { name = ama_fee_percent value = 100 } }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = ama_strategy } } set_variable = { name = ama_strategy value = 0 } }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = ama_wallet } } set_variable = { name = ama_wallet value = 0 } }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = ama_upkeep_percent } } set_variable = { name = ama_upkeep_percent value = 50 } }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = ama_generation } } set_variable = { name = ama_generation value = 0 } }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = ama_tx_sequence } } set_variable = { name = ama_tx_sequence value = 0 } }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = ama_cursor } } set_variable = { name = ama_cursor value = 0 } }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = ama_status } } set_variable = { name = ama_status value = 0 } }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = ama_remaining } } set_variable = { name = ama_remaining value = 0 } }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = ama_month_done } } set_variable = { name = ama_month_done value = 0 } }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = ama_completed } } set_variable = { name = ama_completed value = 0 } }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = ama_last_type } } set_variable = { name = ama_last_type value = 0 } }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = ama_last_mode } } set_variable = { name = ama_last_mode value = 0 } }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = ama_origin_cursor } } set_variable = { name = ama_origin_cursor value = 0 } }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = ama_size_ticket } } set_variable = { name = ama_size_ticket value = 0 } }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = ama_size_phase } } set_variable = { name = ama_size_phase value = 0 } }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = ama_size_attempts } } set_variable = { name = ama_size_attempts value = 0 } }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = ama_size_skips } } set_variable = { name = ama_size_skips value = 0 } }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = ama_size_retry } } set_variable = { name = ama_size_retry value = 0 } }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = ama_size_timeouts } } set_variable = { name = ama_size_timeouts value = 0 } }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = ama_size_last_current } } set_variable = { name = ama_size_last_current value = -1 } }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = ama_size_last_max } } set_variable = { name = ama_size_last_max value = -1 } }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = ama_size_last_state } } set_variable = { name = ama_size_last_state value = 0 } }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = ama_replace_enabled } } set_variable = { name = ama_replace_enabled value = 0 } }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = ama_repl_done } } set_variable = { name = ama_repl_done value = 0 } }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = ama_repl_ticket } } set_variable = { name = ama_repl_ticket value = 0 } }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = ama_repl_phase } } set_variable = { name = ama_repl_phase value = 0 } }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = ama_repl_last_state } } set_variable = { name = ama_repl_last_state value = 0 } }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = ama_reserve_gold } } set_variable = { name = ama_reserve_gold value = 500 } }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = ama_reserve_treasury } } set_variable = { name = ama_reserve_treasury value = 500 } }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = ama_reserve_prestige } } set_variable = { name = ama_reserve_prestige value = 0 } }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = ama_reserve_piety } } set_variable = { name = ama_reserve_piety value = 0 } }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = ama_reserve_barter_goods } } set_variable = { name = ama_reserve_barter_goods value = 0 } }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = ama_target_archer_cavalry } } set_variable = { name = ama_target_archer_cavalry value = 0 } }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = ama_target_archers } } set_variable = { name = ama_target_archers value = 0 } }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = ama_target_camel_cavalry } } set_variable = { name = ama_target_camel_cavalry value = 0 } }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = ama_target_elephant_cavalry } } set_variable = { name = ama_target_elephant_cavalry value = 0 } }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = ama_target_gunpowder } } set_variable = { name = ama_target_gunpowder value = 0 } }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = ama_target_heavy_cavalry } } set_variable = { name = ama_target_heavy_cavalry value = 0 } }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = ama_target_heavy_infantry } } set_variable = { name = ama_target_heavy_infantry value = 0 } }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = ama_target_light_cavalry } } set_variable = { name = ama_target_light_cavalry value = 0 } }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = ama_target_pikemen } } set_variable = { name = ama_target_pikemen value = 0 } }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = ama_target_siege_weapon } } set_variable = { name = ama_target_siege_weapon value = 0 } }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = ama_target_skirmishers } } set_variable = { name = ama_target_skirmishers value = 0 } }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = ama_modes_initialized } }
        set_variable = { name = ama_create_enabled value = 0 }
        set_variable = { name = ama_expand_enabled value = 0 }
        set_variable = { name = ama_title_enabled value = 0 }
        if = { limit = { has_variable = ama_enabled }
            set_variable = { name = ama_create_enabled value = 1 }
            set_variable = { name = ama_expand_enabled value = 1 }
        }
        set_variable = ama_modes_initialized
    }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = ama_sources_initialized } }
        if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = ama_title_create_enabled } }
            set_variable = { name = ama_title_create_enabled value = 0 }
        }
        if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = ama_title_expand_enabled } }
            set_variable = { name = ama_title_expand_enabled value = 0 }
        }
        set_variable = ama_sources_initialized
    }
    ama_refresh_modes_effect = yes
    set_variable = ama_configured
    ama_refresh_title_availability_effect = yes
    ama_recalculate_plan_effect = yes
}
ama_refresh_modes_effect = {
    if = { limit = { OR = {
                var:ama_create_enabled = 1 var:ama_expand_enabled = 1
                AND = { var:ama_title_expand_enabled = 1 ama_title_expand_available_trigger = yes }
                AND = { var:ama_title_create_enabled = 1 ama_title_create_available_trigger = yes }
            } }
        set_variable = ama_enabled
    }
    else = { remove_variable = ama_enabled }
    set_variable = { name = ama_title_enabled value = 0 }
    if = { limit = { OR = { var:ama_title_create_enabled = 1 var:ama_title_expand_enabled = 1 } }
        set_variable = { name = ama_title_enabled value = 1 }
    }
}
ama_open_effect = {
    if = { limit = { ama_player_trigger = yes }
        ama_initialize_effect = yes
        set_variable = ama_panel_open
        ama_schedule_effect = yes
        if = { limit = { has_variable = ama_rule_show } ama_rule_refresh_effect = yes }
    }
}
ama_schedule_effect = {
    if = { limit = { ama_clock_needed_trigger = yes NOT = { has_variable = ama_clock_pending } }
        change_variable = { name = ama_generation add = 1 }
        set_variable = ama_clock_pending
        save_scope_value_as = { name = ama_clock_ticket value = var:ama_generation }
        trigger_event = { on_action = ama_monthly_check months = 1 }
        trigger_event = { on_action = ama_weekly_review days = 7 }
    }
}
ama_restart_effect = {
    ama_size_cancel_effect = yes
    remove_variable = ama_clock_pending
    change_variable = { name = ama_generation add = 1 }
    ama_schedule_effect = yes
}
ama_begin_effect = {
    if = { limit = { ama_can_check_trigger = yes }
        remove_variable = ama_rt_ready
        clear_variable_list = ama_size_blocked
        set_variable = { name = ama_size_attempts value = 0 }
        set_variable = { name = ama_size_skips value = 0 }
        set_variable = { name = ama_size_timeouts value = 0 }
        if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = ama_month_done } } set_variable = { name = ama_month_done value = 0 } }
        set_variable = { name = ama_remaining value = ama_monthly_remaining_value }
        set_variable = { name = ama_completed value = 0 }
        set_variable = { name = ama_repl_done value = 0 }
        if = { limit = { var:ama_remaining > 0 } ama_step_effect = yes }
        else = { set_variable = { name = ama_status value = 12 } }
    }
}
ama_inherit_effect = {
    ama_initialize_effect = yes
    set_variable = { name = ama_fee_percent value = scope:previous_player_character.var:ama_fee_percent }
    set_variable = { name = ama_strategy value = scope:previous_player_character.var:ama_strategy }
    set_variable = { name = ama_wallet value = scope:previous_player_character.var:ama_wallet }
    set_variable = { name = ama_upkeep_percent value = scope:previous_player_character.var:ama_upkeep_percent }
    set_variable = { name = ama_cursor value = scope:previous_player_character.var:ama_cursor }
    set_variable = { name = ama_replace_enabled value = scope:previous_player_character.var:ama_replace_enabled }
    set_variable = { name = ama_reserve_gold value = scope:previous_player_character.var:ama_reserve_gold }
    set_variable = { name = ama_reserve_treasury value = scope:previous_player_character.var:ama_reserve_treasury }
    set_variable = { name = ama_reserve_prestige value = scope:previous_player_character.var:ama_reserve_prestige }
    set_variable = { name = ama_reserve_piety value = scope:previous_player_character.var:ama_reserve_piety }
    set_variable = { name = ama_reserve_barter_goods value = scope:previous_player_character.var:ama_reserve_barter_goods }
    set_variable = { name = ama_target_archer_cavalry value = scope:previous_player_character.var:ama_target_archer_cavalry }
    set_variable = { name = ama_target_archers value = scope:previous_player_character.var:ama_target_archers }
    set_variable = { name = ama_target_camel_cavalry value = scope:previous_player_character.var:ama_target_camel_cavalry }
    set_variable = { name = ama_target_elephant_cavalry value = scope:previous_player_character.var:ama_target_elephant_cavalry }
    set_variable = { name = ama_target_gunpowder value = scope:previous_player_character.var:ama_target_gunpowder }
    set_variable = { name = ama_target_heavy_cavalry value = scope:previous_player_character.var:ama_target_heavy_cavalry }
    set_variable = { name = ama_target_heavy_infantry value = scope:previous_player_character.var:ama_target_heavy_infantry }
    set_variable = { name = ama_target_light_cavalry value = scope:previous_player_character.var:ama_target_light_cavalry }
    set_variable = { name = ama_target_pikemen value = scope:previous_player_character.var:ama_target_pikemen }
    set_variable = { name = ama_target_siege_weapon value = scope:previous_player_character.var:ama_target_siege_weapon }
    set_variable = { name = ama_target_skirmishers value = scope:previous_player_character.var:ama_target_skirmishers }
    if = { limit = { scope:previous_player_character = { has_variable = ama_origin_cursor } }
        set_variable = { name = ama_origin_cursor value = scope:previous_player_character.var:ama_origin_cursor }
    }
    set_variable = { name = ama_title_create_enabled value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = ama_title_expand_enabled value = 0 }
    if = { limit = { scope:previous_player_character = { has_variable = ama_modes_initialized } }
        set_variable = { name = ama_create_enabled value = scope:previous_player_character.var:ama_create_enabled }
        set_variable = { name = ama_expand_enabled value = scope:previous_player_character.var:ama_expand_enabled }
        set_variable = { name = ama_title_enabled value = scope:previous_player_character.var:ama_title_enabled }
        if = { limit = { scope:previous_player_character = { has_variable = ama_sources_initialized } }
            set_variable = { name = ama_title_create_enabled value = scope:previous_player_character.var:ama_title_create_enabled }
            set_variable = { name = ama_title_expand_enabled value = scope:previous_player_character.var:ama_title_expand_enabled }
        }
        else_if = { limit = { var:ama_title_enabled = 1 }
            set_variable = { name = ama_title_create_enabled value = var:ama_create_enabled }
            set_variable = { name = ama_title_expand_enabled value = var:ama_expand_enabled }
        }
    }
    else = {
        set_variable = { name = ama_create_enabled value = 0 }
        set_variable = { name = ama_expand_enabled value = 0 }
        set_variable = { name = ama_title_enabled value = 0 }
        if = { limit = { scope:previous_player_character = { has_variable = ama_enabled } }
            set_variable = { name = ama_create_enabled value = 1 }
            set_variable = { name = ama_expand_enabled value = 1 }
        }
    }
    ama_refresh_modes_effect = yes
    ama_recalculate_plan_effect = yes
    ama_restart_effect = yes
}
ama_recalculate_plan_effect = {
    set_variable = { name = ama_plan_limit value = maa_regiments_max_count }
    set_variable = { name = ama_plan_used value = maa_regiments_count }
    set_variable = { name = ama_plan_weight value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = ama_plan_selected_count value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = ama_plan_archer_cavalry value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = ama_plan_remainder_archer_cavalry value = -1 }
    set_variable = { name = ama_plan_count_archer_cavalry value = ama_count_archer_cavalry_value }
    if = { limit = { var:ama_target_archer_cavalry > 0 }
        change_variable = { name = ama_plan_weight add = var:ama_target_archer_cavalry }
        change_variable = { name = ama_plan_selected_count add = var:ama_plan_count_archer_cavalry }
    }
    set_variable = { name = ama_plan_archers value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = ama_plan_remainder_archers value = -1 }
    set_variable = { name = ama_plan_count_archers value = ama_count_archers_value }
    if = { limit = { var:ama_target_archers > 0 }
        change_variable = { name = ama_plan_weight add = var:ama_target_archers }
        change_variable = { name = ama_plan_selected_count add = var:ama_plan_count_archers }
    }
    set_variable = { name = ama_plan_camel_cavalry value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = ama_plan_remainder_camel_cavalry value = -1 }
    set_variable = { name = ama_plan_count_camel_cavalry value = ama_count_camel_cavalry_value }
    if = { limit = { var:ama_target_camel_cavalry > 0 }
        change_variable = { name = ama_plan_weight add = var:ama_target_camel_cavalry }
        change_variable = { name = ama_plan_selected_count add = var:ama_plan_count_camel_cavalry }
    }
    set_variable = { name = ama_plan_elephant_cavalry value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = ama_plan_remainder_elephant_cavalry value = -1 }
    set_variable = { name = ama_plan_count_elephant_cavalry value = ama_count_elephant_cavalry_value }
    if = { limit = { var:ama_target_elephant_cavalry > 0 }
        change_variable = { name = ama_plan_weight add = var:ama_target_elephant_cavalry }
        change_variable = { name = ama_plan_selected_count add = var:ama_plan_count_elephant_cavalry }
    }
    set_variable = { name = ama_plan_gunpowder value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = ama_plan_remainder_gunpowder value = -1 }
    set_variable = { name = ama_plan_count_gunpowder value = ama_count_gunpowder_value }
    if = { limit = { var:ama_target_gunpowder > 0 }
        change_variable = { name = ama_plan_weight add = var:ama_target_gunpowder }
        change_variable = { name = ama_plan_selected_count add = var:ama_plan_count_gunpowder }
    }
    set_variable = { name = ama_plan_heavy_cavalry value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = ama_plan_remainder_heavy_cavalry value = -1 }
    set_variable = { name = ama_plan_count_heavy_cavalry value = ama_count_heavy_cavalry_value }
    if = { limit = { var:ama_target_heavy_cavalry > 0 }
        change_variable = { name = ama_plan_weight add = var:ama_target_heavy_cavalry }
        change_variable = { name = ama_plan_selected_count add = var:ama_plan_count_heavy_cavalry }
    }
    set_variable = { name = ama_plan_heavy_infantry value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = ama_plan_remainder_heavy_infantry value = -1 }
    set_variable = { name = ama_plan_count_heavy_infantry value = ama_count_heavy_infantry_value }
    if = { limit = { var:ama_target_heavy_infantry > 0 }
        change_variable = { name = ama_plan_weight add = var:ama_target_heavy_infantry }
        change_variable = { name = ama_plan_selected_count add = var:ama_plan_count_heavy_infantry }
    }
    set_variable = { name = ama_plan_light_cavalry value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = ama_plan_remainder_light_cavalry value = -1 }
    set_variable = { name = ama_plan_count_light_cavalry value = ama_count_light_cavalry_value }
    if = { limit = { var:ama_target_light_cavalry > 0 }
        change_variable = { name = ama_plan_weight add = var:ama_target_light_cavalry }
        change_variable = { name = ama_plan_selected_count add = var:ama_plan_count_light_cavalry }
    }
    set_variable = { name = ama_plan_pikemen value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = ama_plan_remainder_pikemen value = -1 }
    set_variable = { name = ama_plan_count_pikemen value = ama_count_pikemen_value }
    if = { limit = { var:ama_target_pikemen > 0 }
        change_variable = { name = ama_plan_weight add = var:ama_target_pikemen }
        change_variable = { name = ama_plan_selected_count add = var:ama_plan_count_pikemen }
    }
    set_variable = { name = ama_plan_siege_weapon value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = ama_plan_remainder_siege_weapon value = -1 }
    set_variable = { name = ama_plan_count_siege_weapon value = ama_count_siege_weapon_value }
    if = { limit = { var:ama_target_siege_weapon > 0 }
        change_variable = { name = ama_plan_weight add = var:ama_target_siege_weapon }
        change_variable = { name = ama_plan_selected_count add = var:ama_plan_count_siege_weapon }
    }
    set_variable = { name = ama_plan_skirmishers value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = ama_plan_remainder_skirmishers value = -1 }
    set_variable = { name = ama_plan_count_skirmishers value = ama_count_skirmishers_value }
    if = { limit = { var:ama_target_skirmishers > 0 }
        change_variable = { name = ama_plan_weight add = var:ama_target_skirmishers }
        change_variable = { name = ama_plan_selected_count add = var:ama_plan_count_skirmishers }
    }
    set_variable = { name = ama_plan_unmanaged value = { value = var:ama_plan_used subtract = var:ama_plan_selected_count min = 0 } }
    set_variable = { name = ama_plan_capacity value = { value = var:ama_plan_limit subtract = var:ama_plan_unmanaged min = 0 floor = yes } }
    set_variable = { name = ama_plan_unassigned value = var:ama_plan_capacity }
    if = { limit = { var:ama_plan_weight > 0 }
        if = { limit = { var:ama_target_archer_cavalry > 0 }
            set_variable = { name = ama_plan_archer_cavalry value = { value = var:ama_plan_capacity multiply = var:ama_target_archer_cavalry divide = var:ama_plan_weight floor = yes } }
            set_variable = { name = ama_plan_remainder_archer_cavalry value = { value = var:ama_plan_capacity multiply = var:ama_target_archer_cavalry modulo = var:ama_plan_weight } }
            change_variable = { name = ama_plan_unassigned subtract = var:ama_plan_archer_cavalry }
        }
        if = { limit = { var:ama_target_archers > 0 }
            set_variable = { name = ama_plan_archers value = { value = var:ama_plan_capacity multiply = var:ama_target_archers divide = var:ama_plan_weight floor = yes } }
            set_variable = { name = ama_plan_remainder_archers value = { value = var:ama_plan_capacity multiply = var:ama_target_archers modulo = var:ama_plan_weight } }
            change_variable = { name = ama_plan_unassigned subtract = var:ama_plan_archers }
        }
        if = { limit = { var:ama_target_camel_cavalry > 0 }
            set_variable = { name = ama_plan_camel_cavalry value = { value = var:ama_plan_capacity multiply = var:ama_target_camel_cavalry divide = var:ama_plan_weight floor = yes } }
            set_variable = { name = ama_plan_remainder_camel_cavalry value = { value = var:ama_plan_capacity multiply = var:ama_target_camel_cavalry modulo = var:ama_plan_weight } }
            change_variable = { name = ama_plan_unassigned subtract = var:ama_plan_camel_cavalry }
        }
        if = { limit = { var:ama_target_elephant_cavalry > 0 }
            set_variable = { name = ama_plan_elephant_cavalry value = { value = var:ama_plan_capacity multiply = var:ama_target_elephant_cavalry divide = var:ama_plan_weight floor = yes } }
            set_variable = { name = ama_plan_remainder_elephant_cavalry value = { value = var:ama_plan_capacity multiply = var:ama_target_elephant_cavalry modulo = var:ama_plan_weight } }
            change_variable = { name = ama_plan_unassigned subtract = var:ama_plan_elephant_cavalry }
        }
        if = { limit = { var:ama_target_gunpowder > 0 }
            set_variable = { name = ama_plan_gunpowder value = { value = var:ama_plan_capacity multiply = var:ama_target_gunpowder divide = var:ama_plan_weight floor = yes } }
            set_variable = { name = ama_plan_remainder_gunpowder value = { value = var:ama_plan_capacity multiply = var:ama_target_gunpowder modulo = var:ama_plan_weight } }
            change_variable = { name = ama_plan_unassigned subtract = var:ama_plan_gunpowder }
        }
        if = { limit = { var:ama_target_heavy_cavalry > 0 }
            set_variable = { name = ama_plan_heavy_cavalry value = { value = var:ama_plan_capacity multiply = var:ama_target_heavy_cavalry divide = var:ama_plan_weight floor = yes } }
            set_variable = { name = ama_plan_remainder_heavy_cavalry value = { value = var:ama_plan_capacity multiply = var:ama_target_heavy_cavalry modulo = var:ama_plan_weight } }
            change_variable = { name = ama_plan_unassigned subtract = var:ama_plan_heavy_cavalry }
        }
        if = { limit = { var:ama_target_heavy_infantry > 0 }
            set_variable = { name = ama_plan_heavy_infantry value = { value = var:ama_plan_capacity multiply = var:ama_target_heavy_infantry divide = var:ama_plan_weight floor = yes } }
            set_variable = { name = ama_plan_remainder_heavy_infantry value = { value = var:ama_plan_capacity multiply = var:ama_target_heavy_infantry modulo = var:ama_plan_weight } }
            change_variable = { name = ama_plan_unassigned subtract = var:ama_plan_heavy_infantry }
        }

                change_variable = { name = ama_plan_light_cavalry add = 1 }
                change_variable = { name = ama_plan_unassigned subtract = 1 }
                set_variable = { name = ama_plan_remainder_light_cavalry value = -1 }
            }
            if = { limit = { var:ama_plan_remainder_category = 9 }
                change_variable = { name = ama_plan_pikemen add = 1 }
                change_variable = { name = ama_plan_unassigned subtract = 1 }
                set_variable = { name = ama_plan_remainder_pikemen value = -1 }
            }
            if = { limit = { var:ama_plan_remainder_category = 10 }
                change_variable = { name = ama_plan_siege_weapon add = 1 }
                change_variable = { name = ama_plan_unassigned subtract = 1 }
                set_variable = { name = ama_plan_remainder_siege_weapon value = -1 }
            }
            if = { limit = { var:ama_plan_remainder_category = 11 }
                change_variable = { name = ama_plan_skirmishers add = 1 }
                change_variable = { name = ama_plan_unassigned subtract = 1 }
                set_variable = { name = ama_plan_remainder_skirmishers value = -1 }
            }
        }
        if = { limit = { var:ama_plan_unassigned > 0 }
            set_variable = { name = ama_plan_best_remainder value = -1 }
            set_variable = { name = ama_plan_remainder_category value = 0 }
            if = { limit = { var:ama_target_archer_cavalry > 0 var:ama_plan_remainder_archer_cavalry > var:ama_plan_best_remainder }
                set_variable = { name = ama_plan_best_remainder value = var:ama_plan_remainder_archer_cavalry }
                set_variable = { name = ama_plan_remainder_category value = 1 }
            }
            if = { limit = { var:ama_target_archers > 0 var:ama_plan_remainder_archers > var:ama_plan_best_remainder }
                set_variable = { name = ama_plan_best_remainder value = var:ama_plan_remainder_archers }
                set_variable = { name = ama_plan_remainder_category value = 2 }
            }
            if = { limit = { var:ama_target_camel_cavalry > 0 var:ama_plan_remainder_camel_cavalry > var:ama_plan_best_remainder }
                set_variable = { name = ama_plan_best_remainder value = var:ama_plan_remainder_camel_cavalry }
                set_variable = { name = ama_plan_remainder_category value = 3 }
            }
            if = { limit = { var:ama_target_elephant_cavalry > 0 var:ama_plan_remainder_elephant_cavalry > var:ama_plan_best_remainder }
                set_variable = { name = ama_plan_best_remainder value = var:ama_plan_remainder_elephant_cavalry }
                set_variable = { name = ama_plan_remainder_category value = 4 }
            }
            if = { limit = { var:ama_target_gunpowder > 0 var:ama_plan_remainder_gunpowder > var:ama_plan_best_remainder }
                set_variable = { name = ama_plan_best_remainder value = var:ama_plan_remainder_gunpowder }
                set_variable = { name = ama_plan_remainder_category value = 5 }
            }
            if = { limit = { var:ama_target_heavy_cavalry > 0 var:ama_plan_remainder_heavy_cavalry > var:ama_plan_best_remainder }
                set_variable = { name = ama_plan_best_remainder value = var:ama_plan_remainder_heavy_cavalry }
                set_variable = { name = ama_plan_remainder_category value = 6 }
            }
            if = { limit = { var:ama_target_heavy_infantry > 0 var:ama_plan_remainder_heavy_infantry > var:ama_plan_best_remainder }

                change_variable = { name = ama_plan_unassigned subtract = 1 }

            }
            if = { limit = { var:ama_plan_remainder_category = 2 }
                change_variable = { name = ama_plan_archers add = 1 }
                change_variable = { name = ama_plan_unassigned subtract = 1 }
                set_variable = { name = ama_plan_remainder_archers value = -1 }
            }
            if = { limit = { var:ama_plan_remainder_category = 3 }
                change_variable = { name = ama_plan_camel_cavalry add = 1 }
                change_variable = { name = ama_plan_unassigned subtract = 1 }
                set_variable = { name = ama_plan_remainder_camel_cavalry value = -1 }
            }
            if = { limit = { var:ama_plan_remainder_category = 4 }

                change_variable = { name = ama_plan_unassigned subtract = 1 }

                set_variable = { name = ama_plan_remainder_heavy_infantry value = -1 }

                change_variable = { name = ama_plan_unassigned subtract = 1 }
                set_variable = { name = ama_plan_remainder_pikemen value = -1 }

                set_variable = { name = ama_plan_remainder_skirmishers value = -1 }

        }
    }
    else = {
        set_variable = { name = ama_plan_capacity value = 0 }
        set_variable = { name = ama_plan_unassigned value = 0 }
    }
}
ama_rule_refresh_effect = {
    if = { limit = { ama_rule_actor_trigger = yes }
        save_scope_as = ama_rule_owner
        set_variable = { name = ama_rule_titles value = 0 }
        set_variable = { name = ama_rule_common_laws value = ama_rule_laws_value }
        set_variable = { name = ama_rule_common_contracts value = ama_rule_contracts_value }
        set_variable = { name = ama_rule_common_timed value = ama_rule_timed_value }
        set_variable = { name = ama_rule_common_cycle value = ama_rule_cycle_value }
        set_variable = { name = ama_rule_common_pending_house value = ama_rule_pending_house_value }
        set_variable = { name = ama_rule_common_pending_building value = ama_rule_pending_building_value }
        every_held_title = { limit = { holder = scope:ama_rule_owner }
            set_variable = { name = ama_rule_visible value = 0 }
            if = { limit = { holder = scope:ama_rule_owner tier >= tier_county ama_rule_title_candidate_trigger = yes }
                set_variable = { name = ama_rule_owner value = scope:ama_rule_owner }
                set_variable = { name = ama_rule_base value = ama_rule_base_value }
                set_variable = { name = ama_rule_laws value = scope:ama_rule_owner.var:ama_rule_common_laws }
                set_variable = { name = ama_rule_contracts value = scope:ama_rule_owner.var:ama_rule_common_contracts }
                set_variable = { name = ama_rule_timed value = scope:ama_rule_owner.var:ama_rule_common_timed }
                set_variable = { name = ama_rule_cycle value = scope:ama_rule_owner.var:ama_rule_common_cycle }
                set_variable = { name = ama_rule_pending_house value = scope:ama_rule_owner.var:ama_rule_common_pending_house }
                set_variable = { name = ama_rule_pending_building value = scope:ama_rule_owner.var:ama_rule_common_pending_building }
                set_variable = { name = ama_rule_pending value = { value = var:ama_rule_pending_house add = var:ama_rule_pending_building } }
                set_variable = { name = ama_rule_subtotal value = { value = var:ama_rule_base
                        add = var:ama_rule_laws add = var:ama_rule_contracts add = var:ama_rule_timed add = var:ama_rule_cycle } }
                set_variable = { name = ama_rule_estimate value = -1 }
                if = { limit = { var:ama_rule_pending = 0 var:ama_rule_base >= 0 }
                    set_variable = { name = ama_rule_estimate value = { value = var:ama_rule_subtotal min = 0 floor = yes } }
                }
                set_variable = { name = ama_rule_used value = ama_rule_owned_count_value }
                set_variable = { name = ama_rule_weight value = 0 }
                set_variable = { name = ama_rule_selected value = 0 }
                set_variable = { name = ama_rule_weight_archer_cavalry value = scope:ama_rule_owner.var:ama_target_archer_cavalry }
                set_variable = { name = ama_rule_count_archer_cavalry value = ama_title_count_archer_cavalry_value }
                set_variable = { name = ama_rule_plan_archer_cavalry value = 0 }
                set_variable = { name = ama_rule_rem_archer_cavalry value = -1 }
                if = { limit = { var:ama_rule_weight_archer_cavalry > 0 }
                    change_variable = { name = ama_rule_weight add = var:ama_rule_weight_archer_cavalry }
                    change_variable = { name = ama_rule_selected add = var:ama_rule_count_archer_cavalry }
                }
                set_variable = { name = ama_rule_weight_archers value = scope:ama_rule_owner.var:ama_target_archers }

                set_variable = { name = ama_rule_plan_camel_cavalry value = 0 }

ama_pick_reset_effect = {
    set_variable = { name = ama_pick_best_score value = -1 }
    set_variable = { name = ama_pick_best_type value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = ama_pick_best_cost value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = ama_pick_best_upkeep value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = ama_pick_best_affordable value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = ama_pick_best_quality value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = ama_pick_best_siege value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = ama_pick_best_pursuit value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = ama_pick_best_screen value = 0 }
}
ama_pick_1_effect = {
    ama_quote_1_effect = yes
    if = { limit = { OR = { var:ama_strategy = 1 ama_affordable_trigger = yes } }
        set_variable = { name = ama_pick_score value = ama_quality_abudrar_value }
        set_variable = { name = ama_pick_quality value = ama_quality_abudrar_value }
        set_variable = { name = ama_pick_siege value = var:ama_rt_siege_1 }
        set_variable = { name = ama_pick_pursuit value = var:ama_rt_pursuit_1 }
        set_variable = { name = ama_pick_screen value = var:ama_rt_screen_1 }
        if = { limit = { var:ama_strategy = 0 } set_variable = { name = ama_pick_score value = ama_value_abudrar_value } }
        set_variable = { name = ama_pick_cost value = ama_pick_cost_value }
        set_variable = { name = ama_pick_upkeep value = ama_pick_upkeep_abudrar_value }
        if = { limit = { ama_pick_better_trigger = yes }
            set_variable = { name = ama_pick_best_type value = 1 }
            set_variable = { name = ama_pick_best_score value = var:ama_pick_score }
            set_variable = { name = ama_pick_best_cost value = var:ama_pick_cost }
            set_variable = { name = ama_pick_best_upkeep value = var:ama_pick_upkeep }
            set_variable = { name = ama_pick_best_quality value = var:ama_pick_quality }
            set_variable = { name = ama_pick_best_siege value = var:ama_pick_siege }
            set_variable = { name = ama_pick_best_pursuit value = var:ama_pick_pursuit }
            set_variable = { name = ama_pick_best_screen value = var:ama_pick_screen }
            set_variable = { name = ama_pick_best_affordable value = 0 }
            if = { limit = { ama_affordable_trigger = yes } set_variable = { name = ama_pick_best_affordable value = 1 } }
        }
    }

    if = { limit = { OR = { var:ama_strategy = 1 ama_affordable_trigger = yes } }
        set_variable = { name = ama_pick_score value = ama_quality_crossbowmen_value }
        set_variable = { name = ama_pick_quality value = ama_quality_crossbowmen_value }
        set_variable = { name = ama_pick_siege value = var:ama_rt_siege_17 }
        set_variable = { name = ama_pick_pursuit value = var:ama_rt_pursuit_17 }
        set_variable = { name = ama_pick_screen value = var:ama_rt_screen_17 }
        if = { limit = { var:ama_strategy = 0 } set_variable = { name = ama_pick_score value = ama_value_crossbowmen_value } }
        set_variable = { name = ama_pick_cost value = ama_pick_cost_value }
        set_variable = { name = ama_pick_upkeep value = ama_pick_upkeep_crossbowmen_value }
        if = { limit = { ama_pick_better_trigger = yes }
            set_variable = { name = ama_pick_best_type value = 17 }
            set_variable = { name = ama_pick_best_score value = var:ama_pick_score }
            set_variable = { name = ama_pick_best_cost value = var:ama_pick_cost }
            set_variable = { name = ama_pick_best_upkeep value = var:ama_pick_upkeep }
            set_variable = { name = ama_pick_best_quality value = var:ama_pick_quality }
            set_variable = { name = ama_pick_best_siege value = var:ama_pick_siege }
            set_variable = { name = ama_pick_best_pursuit value = var:ama_pick_pursuit }
            set_variable = { name = ama_pick_best_screen value = var:ama_pick_screen }
